Here's the updated prompt — same surreal coastal Mediterranean composition and dreamy mood, now featuring a young man instead of a boy:

Surreal cinematic portrait photograph, shot on medium-format film camera (Mamiya 7 II or Pentax 67) with 80mm lens, Kodak Portra 400 film, soft natural Mediterranean afternoon daylight, dreamy painterly color grade with cool ocean blue background and warm earthy skin tones, surrealist documentary aesthetic, Ryan McGinley meets Slim Aarons coastal-dreamlike mood.
A close-up surreal portrait of a young European man in his mid-twenties, captured from behind and slightly to the side at a three-quarter back angle. He's positioned just at the water's edge of a calm Mediterranean sea or coastal harbor, with the soft out-of-focus turquoise-blue ocean filling the entire background of the frame as a smooth painterly gradient.
He has wet sun-bleached light golden-brown hair, slightly tousled and damp from swimming, individual strands sticking together in wet clumps that fall messily around his ears, the back of his neck, and along his temples. The hair has natural sun-kissed highlights and a slightly grown-out cut showing recent ocean swimming. A glimpse of his face in profile is visible on the right side of the frame — a sharp masculine cheekbone with light freckles and faint stubble along his jawline, the corner of one closed or downcast eye with thick natural lashes, the bridge of his straight nose, the corner of full lips slightly parted in a quiet contemplative expression. His skin is sun-tanned warm honey-bronze with subtle red flush from the summer sun, a small earring or stud catching the light at his earlobe. Just his cheek, jawline, ear, and the upper part of his bare muscular shoulders are visible at the bottom edge of the frame, with small water droplets still clinging to his neck and the curve of his shoulder. A delicate thin gold chain necklace is just visible around his neck.
Draped dramatically across the top of his head and tumbling down the side of his face is a freshly caught raw octopus — a real cephalopod with a soft pinkish-coral mantle (the bulbous head) sitting balanced on top of his hair like an absurd surreal hat, and multiple long muscular tentacles cascading downward in organic spiral curves. The tentacles are a beautiful gradient of warm amber, peachy-pink, and deep umber-brown tones, the underside revealing rows of perfect circular pale-cream suction cups in tidy lines descending each tentacle. One tentacle drapes down across his cheek and curls toward his jaw line, ending in a small graceful curl near his chin. Another wraps around the side of his head past his ear. The octopus's skin has a wet glossy iridescent sheen catching the soft light, with slight tonal variations of mottled brown spots and pinkish patches. The texture appears organic, soft, slightly translucent in highlights. The whole creature looks freshly caught — natural colors not yet fully faded, still glistening with seawater.
The background is the calm Mediterranean ocean — a vast soft gradient of pale azure-blue at the horizon transitioning into deeper teal-blue closer to the camera, gentle ripples and small wavelets visible but heavily blurred from the shallow depth of field, suggesting a calm bay or harbor. Tiny bright specular highlights sparkle on the distant water surface from the afternoon sun. No horizon line visible, no boats, no distractions — just pure painterly water filling the entire frame as a clean backdrop.
Composition: vertical 4:5 portrait orientation, the man's head positioned slightly off-center toward the right of the frame using rule-of-thirds, the octopus draped dramatically over his head extending into the upper portion of the frame and cascading down across the right edge with its tentacle curling around his face. The wet hair, octopus, and visible cheek/jaw of the man fill the right two-thirds of the frame as the focal subject, the soft blurred ocean background fills the left third as breathing negative space. Close intimate framing brings extreme detail to the texture of both the octopus skin, the wet hair, the stubble, and the human skin. Slight back-angle perspective showing more of the back of his head than his face — emphasizing mystery and surrealism over identification.
Lighting: soft natural overhead-and-slightly-left Mediterranean afternoon daylight, gentle directional but diffused, creating soft highlights on the octopus's wet glossy skin, illuminating the rows of suction cups with delicate shadow detail, catching the wet strands of his hair with subtle rim-light, gently highlighting the contours of his masculine jawline and cheekbone. No harsh shadows — quiet quality of light that feels like a slightly overcast sunny day or open shade by the water. Ambient soft blue fill from the reflective ocean below adds a cool tonal balance against the warm tones of the octopus and skin.
Color palette: cool azure-teal ocean blue background, warm peachy-coral and amber-brown octopus tones, pale cream suction cups, sun-bleached golden-brown wet hair, warm honey-tanned skin tones, glistening highlights of seawater, soft umber and dusky pink mottling on the octopus skin, subtle iridescent sheen, gold chain accent at the neck. Painterly medium-format film grain, soft Portra color rendering with natural saturation, gentle highlight roll-off, subtle warm-cool color contrast between the cool blue background and the warm organic tones of the subject, dreamy surrealist coastal aesthetic — feels like a fine-art editorial spread about Mediterranean fishing culture or a surreal moment captured on a summer day in Greece, Italy, or Portugal. Reminiscent of editorial photography by Cass Bird, Petra Collins, and Sicily-shot fashion editorials that play with surreal natural elements as styling.

The entire rear glass window of the SUV is streaming with water and soap — a pressurized jet of water arcs in from the upper-left of the frame, caught mid-air as crystal-clear thin stream, splashing and hitting the glass, creating bright white rivulets and dense foamy suds that crawl down across the window surface. The glass is half-obscured by the soap pattern but through it, two figures are faintly visible inside the SUV, blurred and distorted by the running water like a dreamlike impressionist painting.

She wears a fitted cream ribbed cotton tank top. Beside her in the passenger seat is a young man with curly dark brown hair, wearing a faded forest-green vintage t-shirt, his face seen from near-profile as he leans closer to her with an amused half-smile. Their interaction reads as an intimate quiet moment — mid-conversation, mid-laugh — while the car is being absurdly washed around them. Their features are softened and watery through the glass, the running water creating a dreamlike distortion effect on both faces.

A coiled royal-blue rubber pressure hose runs from the utility pole toward the SUV. Stained concrete pavement beneath the vehicle darkened with water.
Lighting: harsh direct desert afternoon sunlight from camera-left creating strong warm directional light, casting sharp shadows on the SUV's sky-blue body and highlighting every soap suds bubble and water droplet. The water jet catches pinpoints of sunlight like sparkling diamonds in mid-air. The rear tire casts a deep dark shadow across the soapy tailgate. Warm golden-hour-adjacent light with slight haze of atomized water mist visible in the sunbeams.
Composition: vertical 2:3 portrait orientation, the SUV's rear tailgate and spare tire dominate the right two-thirds of the frame as a chunky graphic visual anchor — the large black tire in the middle-right acts as a strong circular focal point with the two watery human silhouettes visible behind the soapy window to its left. The water jet arcs in from upper-left corner creating a dynamic diagonal line across the upper third. The desert environment opens out into negative space on the left side of the frame, providing contrast and scale. The pale blue of the SUV echoes the pale blue of the distant sky.
Color palette: sun-faded pale sky-blue SUV paint, jet-black tire rubber, silver steel wheel rim, crystalline water droplets and bright white soap foam, tomato-red taillight pop, warm sun-bleached dusty yellow desert, pale hazy blue sky, royal-blue rubber hose accent, cream ribbed top, forest-green t-shirt, dark chocolate-brown hair, porcelain skin tones distorted through the wet glass.
